is blank (`None`/empty/whitespace), else `0`.\n- **Total Errors** = `SLA Breach + Missing Escalation`\n- **Error Summary** exactly one of:\n  - `None`\n  - `SLA Breach`\n  - `Missing Escalation`\n  - `SLA Breach, Missing Escalation`\n\n### Spot-check samples (to confirm correct thresholding)\nExamples verified against SLA rules:\n\n- `TK-002 (P1, Open 6, Max 4, EscReq Y, EscCode None)`  \n  → SLA Breach = 1, Missing Escalation = 1, Total = 2\n\n- `TK-003 (P2, Open 7, Max 8, EscReq Y, EscCode None)`  \n  → SLA Breach = 0, Missing Escalation = 1, Total = 1\n\n- `TK-006 (P3, Open 20, Max 16, EscReq N)`  \n  → SLA Breach = 1, Missing Escalation = 0, Total = 1\n\n- `TK-007 (P4, Open 18, Max 24, EscReq N)`  \n  → SLA Breach = 0, Missing Escalation = 0, Total = 0\n\n---\n\n## Step 3 — Built Grouped by `(Queue, Region)`\n- Included **only** rows where `Total Errors > 0`\n- Sorted by `Queue` ascending, then `Region` ascending\n- Appended final **Grand Total** row with totals across the full dataset\n\n### Grand totals (from Summary final row)\n- **SLA Breaches:** `10`\n- **Missing Escalations:** `6`\n- **Total Errors:** `16`\n\n---\n\n## Step 4 — Saved Excel output\nSaved as:\n\n-
